## Reviewed contract first â€” proposed S3 cohort v4

Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:

1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is â‰¤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_timeâˆ’error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

# Architect answers: LR-01 source-time cohort and money limit (2026-10-15)

Authority: `tasks/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit.md` Q1-Q4, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md`, and `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1.

## Q1 - clock proof and limit

**Accept.** Source-time ordering requires a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped attestation from an identified authority, valid for every reading used, with a proven maximum UTC error no greater than 60 seconds. Widen each source or measurement interval by that error. A simulated attestation is explicitly identified and cannot validate a real source; `source_mode` alone is insufficient. Missing, expired, conflicting, or clock-step-affected proof leaves the series limited. Pin the exact attestation version in the new analytic basis; do not alter old runs. The 60-second bound is a ceiling, not a gateway capability claim.

## Q2 - overlap and epoch

**Accept.** Equal or overlapping uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP` and ambiguous. Gateway sequence resolves identity and duplicates, never an overlapping measurement or source-time order. Do not infer continuity across epochs from timestamps. Retain the current ambiguous default and explicit cohort-selection behavior; any automatic epoch continuity requires a separately reviewed public proof contract.

## Q3 - affected windows

**Accept with scope clarification.** On attestation creation, replacement, expiry, or withdrawal, find accepted readings whose clock basis could change, including readings covered by the old or new validity interval. Enqueue every UTC-day window whose six-hour read bracket intersects their source or measurement intervals. The existing job expansion supplies the neighboring continuity days; do not expand the bracket a second time. Use the same bracket for query and requeue. If either bound changes, version and revise both together. Reassess through existing scoped jobs and append-only selections; preserve old IDs and idempotent replay.

## Q4 - Finding and eligibility

**Accept.** Keep `SUPPORTED` only for proven Candidate spans. A material series refusal that truncates the episode yields a new FindingRevision with typed `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, refused span and evidence/assessment links, and `SERIES_LIMIT` as end reason; retain `OPEN_BOUND` only if independently true. Derive the versioned three-state `money_eligibility` from the latest selected window and latest Finding on each read. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` wins while relevant S3/S4 work or selection staleness remains; after work settles, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` applies to a material unresolved refusal. `ELIGIBLE` passes this gate only: an amount still requires the exact-head `CURRENT PRICED` consequence and existing source/currentness checks. Both S4 Financials and S5 consume the same state and exact lineage. Preserve the recorded 7.5625 USD revision as history without a current-amount label.

## Packet changes for the Planner

1. Make attestation-change requeue cover both old and new validity intervals, affected accepted reading/measurement intervals, and the same six-hour query bracket; name creation, replacement, expiry, and withdrawal in phase 1 proof.
2. Specify the eligibility precedence and that `ELIGIBLE` is only the series/reassessment gate, not a substitute for `CURRENT PRICED` and the existing source/currentness checks. Assert pending, settled-withheld, and eligible cases in phase 2 proof.
3. Make explicit that an epoch may use the existing explicit-selection path, while automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01.

## Follow-up M1 (2026-10-15)

Accept the placement-instant correction; replace the proposed sequence-disagreement test. For an attested channel, order by `placed_at` (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) with +/- the attested UTC error, not by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals within one channel remains `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous. When placement-uncertainty intervals are disjoint, source-time order wins even if gateway admission sequence disagrees: this is a late arrival, not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Gateway sequence still resolves identity and duplicates. This supersedes Q1/Q2's widened-interval wording and preserves ordinary attested five-minute means and the 15.8125 USD positive case.

## Findings

### M1 (Medium, contract soundness): the uncertainty and overlap rule, as written, refuses ordinary in-order series, including the slice's own positive case

Packet "Reviewed contract" item 2 (packet line 40) and Architect question 2.

Item 2 treats each reading as `[source_timeâˆ’error, source_time+error]` "(or the reported measurement interval widened by error)", and says equal or overlapping intervals "remain REPORTS_OVERLAP/ambiguous". The S4 power series is back-to-back 5-minute `INTERVAL_MEAN`s (`s4-power-late-demo-cases-v1.json:10`). Widen each by 60 s and every neighbouring pair overlaps: [06:59,07:06] against [07:04,07:11]. The same happens to point readings taken 2 minutes apart or less. So the rule can make attested on-time data, and the 15.8125 positive variant, ambiguous. Today's rule (`cohort.py:113-136`) flags overlap only for identical `placed_at`, and flags order only where sequence and placement disagree.

- **Why it matters.** This is the reviewed contract that phase 1 implements. Read literally, it regresses ordinary data. Read loosely, the implementer chooses the ordering key. Either way, the Architect is being asked to bless an unsound sentence.
- **Fix.** State the ordering key: `placed_at` (interval end, else source time) Â± error, not the widened measurement interval. Say that abutting interval means are not overlap. Say that uncertainty-interval overlap creates ambiguity only where gateway sequence and source order disagree; where they agree, today's result stands. Add a pure test with dense, in-order attested data that stays usable.

### M2 (Medium, acceptance gap): the attested admission-sequenced case, which is the core of Architect Q1, is neither asserted nor walked

Packet phase 1 tests (packet line 61), phase 2 walkthrough (line 62), and the owner walkthrough (line 64).

Q1's positive rule is that an attested same-epoch stream is placed by source time, so "a later gateway sequence with an earlier source time is a late arrival, not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`". The owner walkthrough shows only two cases:

- run 1 limited and withheld (unattested);
- the on-time-sequenced upload with an attestation, at 15.8125.

The on-time variant does not exercise the new rule at all, because it passes today's sequence check. Phase 1 lists "admission-sequenced run 1" with no expected outcome. Nothing asserts the append-only transition the decision describes: run 1 withheld, then an attestation recorded, then requeue and reassess, then a new selection and FindingRevision, then `ELIGIBLE` at 15.8125, with the 7.5625 revision and the dispatch-3.1 selection kept.

- **Why it matters.** A build could ship the fail-closed half and never prove the reordering the node exists to add. The registry acceptance target requires "verify exact assessment, Finding and consequence lineage" for both variants.
- **Fix.** Add an explicit outcome matrix to phase 1 and phase 2:

  | Case | Expected result |
  | --- | --- |
  | Run 1, unattested | INDETERMINATE, `SERIES_LIMIT`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` |
  | Run 1, attested | Source-ordered, full episode, 15.8125, `ELIGIBLE` |
  | Run 1, attestation added later | Withheld, then pending reassessment, then eligible; old IDs unchanged |
  | On-time variant | 15.8125 |
  | Clock-negative cases | As listed in the packet |

  Add the attested run-1 transition to the owner walkthrough.

### M3 (Medium, fail-closed boundary underspecified): the attestation write route, authority and as-of read are left open

Packet table row 1 (line 47) and contract item 1 (line 39).

The attestation is the one record that turns a withheld amount into current money. Yet the packet leaves its route as "new migration/adapter/API or importer", without:

- who may author it;
- how it is scoped to a tenant and Site;
- whether it is append-only, or can be superseded or revoked;
- whether it is read as of `knowledge_at`.

The existing replay test (`test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py:139-143`) re-derives a past cutoff's `input_sha256`. An attestation read without a `recorded_at <= knowledge_at` filter would change past bases or break that replay. A revoked or superseded attestation must withhold money again; the packet requeues on "attestation changes", but does not say revocation is a change.

- **Fix.**
  - Name one route: for example, a SYSTEM-only, server-authorized fixture or CLI importer for labelled simulated attestations, with no real-source write path in this slice.
  - Refuse gateway-credential and client authorship, consistent with the A-S5-overview P7 Q3 rule.
  - Make the record append-only, with supersession and revocation.
  - Read it as of `knowledge_at`.
  - Requeue on revocation as well, with tests for each.

### M4 (Medium, walkthrough not reachable with the named tools): no UI serving path for the owner checkpoint

Packet phase 2 (line 62) and the owner walkthrough (line 64).

The owner is to view Financials at desktop and narrow width on a private DB. The two named tools are backend-only:

- `s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` reads consequences in-process (`:56,72-91`) and has only `run`/`drop` (`:367`).
- `s402d_tick_walkthrough.py` likewise.

The only `serve` (API plus printed `npm.cmd run dev` commands) is in `s402b_fuel_cost_walkthrough.py:257-268`, and `checked_db` limits it to `assetops_s402b*` databases (docstring `:21-23`). Money also reads current through the API only once the source gate is open; s402b opens it with a Performance comparison (`:7`).

The packet also names no browser-evidence run (as `tools/s402b-fuel-cost-evidence.mjs` was for S4-02b, 90/90 at 1440/390) for a changed owner-visible panel.

- **Fix.**
  - Add a `serve` subcommand (new) to the extended walkthrough, or name another serving route, together with the frontend command and the source-gate precondition.
  - Name a browser-evidence run at 1440 and 390 for the four states (new script, or an extension of the s402b one). Record a skipped run as `not run` with its precondition.

### M5 (Medium, fail-closed gap on the S4 Financials API): consequence read fields still assert a current amount for run 1

`backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:202-214`, served by `GET .../financial-consequences` at `finding_api.py:246-277`.

The packet routes eligibility through the bridge and the panel. But the consequence read still sets these from basis currentness alone:

- `selected.presented_as_current = is_head and state == "CURRENT"`
- `currentness.current_amount_shown`

So for run 1 the S4 Financials API says a current amount is shown while the bridge withholds it. The walkthrough's money predicate (`s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py:91`) uses the same basis test. The packet's own invariant says "Do not rely on a UI-only predicate", and these fields are API-level claims that S5-01 or tools may read.

- **Fix.** Either gate `presented_as_current` and `current_amount_shown` on `money_eligibility` and include eligibility in that response, or rename or document them as basis-currentness only. Then assert run 1 shows false or withheld on both endpoints.

### Low (carries)

- **L1.** The digest citation `assessment_inputs.py:205,334` is off. Line 205 is `watermarks` and line 334 is inside `build_input_set`. The input payload and digest are at about `:280-301` and `sha256_json` at `:305-307`.
- **L2.** The registry node `task` is `"LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"`. Other non-S nodes use the short id (`G-05`, `PRE-S3-04`), and the gate lookup at `delivery_next_work.cjs:247` matches `q.task === node.task`, where the gate id is `LR-01`. This is harmless today, because the global active and in-review stop uses the gate queue. Use `"LR-01"` for consistency.
- **L3.** Name the existing test files to extend: `backend/tests/test_s4_finding_api.py` and `frontend/src/sites/__tests__/siteFinancials.test.tsx`. Add `npx.cmd tsc --noEmit` and the frontend test run to phase 2. `test_s4_consequence_read.py:18-31` covers only display rounding. Phase 2's focused set should also include the S4 consequence, recompute, tick and health files (`TESTING.md:101`), because eligibility reads their state.

Also state whether the Lab-delayed simulated gateway path (`service.py`, the default late path for S2-S7) gets a simulated attestation in this slice or stays limited. That decides whether ordinary simulated late data ever shows money in S5 demos.

## Phasing, tests, owner review

 Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

-**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.

Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. Compute eligibility on every read from persisted lineage, so late evidence immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.

 Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

+## Architect rulings
+
+Architect Q1-Q4 and the corrected overlap rule are accepted in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md` (including “Packet changes for the Planner” 1-3 and “Follow-up M1”) and decision `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. Those rulings govern implementation and proof above.
 
-1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+**Plan fix round 1:** Applied the Architect's placement/overlap and requeue rulings; fixed the attestation authority, append-only as-of path and eligibility precedence; added exact outcome and transition proof across S3, Finding and Financials; made the owner UI serving and browser evidence reachable; corrected citations, test targets, simulated wording and cross-midnight window scope, with expected model/matcher revision churn called out.
 
 **Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

 Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. +2. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. +## Architect rulings
+
+Architect Q1-Q4 and the corrected overlap rule are accepted in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md` (including “Packet changes for the Planner” 1-3 and “Follow-up M1”) and decision `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. Those rulings govern implementation and proof above.
 
-1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.

 Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. +1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

Gate `selected.presented_as_current` and `currentness.current_amount_shown` on it in `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:202-214`; expose eligibility there. S5-01 consumes the same state and exact lineage. |

-**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient.  ## Phases, proof and review
 
+The same outcome table is the acceptance oracle for both phases. IDs below mean exact persisted IDs, never only a count or a currentness label; 7.5625 and 15.8125 are USD consequence amounts for the authored fixture.
+
+| Input / transition | S3 status and limits | Finding revision / end reason | Consequence amount and lineage | `money_eligibility` |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| Admission-sequenced run 1, no attestation, after late acceptance and settled tick | `INDETERMINATE`; `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`, `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` | New revision; proven Candidate spans `SUPPORTED`, material refused tail linked, `SERIES_LIMIT`; `OPEN_BOUND` only if independently true | Recorded 7.5625 revision and its exact old run/selection/Finding/consequence IDs retained; no current amount | `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` |
+| Same run 1, valid labelled simulated attestation recorded, before reassessment settles | Prior selection is stale; relevant S3/S4 work pending | Prior revision retained, new result pending | 7.5625 and all old IDs retained; no current amount | `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` |
+| Same run 1, reassessment settled under valid simulated attestation | Source-ordered full series; no generator-series refusal; late admission sequence is not inversion | New full-episode `SUPPORTED` revision with truthful non-series end reason | New exact-head 15.8125 revision with new run/selection/Finding/consequence IDs; old 7.5625 and IDs remain historical | `ELIGIBLE`, subject to exact-head `CURRENT PRICED` and source/currentness checks |
+| On-time-sequenced late upload with valid simulated attestation | Full ordered series, no generator-series refusal | Full-episode `SUPPORTED` revision, truthful end | Exact-head 15.8125 with all linked IDs | `ELIGIBLE`, subject to the same amount checks |
+| Simulated data without attestation; expired, conflicting, revoked, clock-step or genuine-overlap proof | Relevant typed series/overlap limit; no invented order | Material limit yields revised `SERIES_LIMIT`; pending work takes precedence | Preserve previous revisions/IDs; no current amount | `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` until settled, then `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` for material refusal |

+## Architect rulings
+
+Architect Q1-Q4 and the corrected overlap rule are accepted in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md` (including “Packet changes for the Planner” 1-3 and “Follow-up M1”) and decision `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. Those rulings govern implementation and proof above.
 
-1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+**Plan fix round 1:** Applied the Architect's placement/overlap and requeue rulings; fixed the attestation authority, append-only as-of path and eligibility precedence; added exact outcome and transition proof across S3, Finding and Financials; made the owner UI serving and browser evidence reachable; corrected citations, test targets, simulated wording and cross-midnight window scope, with expected model/matcher revision churn called out.
 
 **Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

 Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.

Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.

Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

 ## Phases, proof and review
 
+The same outcome table is the acceptance oracle for both phases. IDs below mean exact persisted IDs, never only a count or a currentness label; 7.5625 and 15.8125 are USD consequence amounts for the authored fixture.
+
+| Input / transition | S3 status and limits | Finding revision / end reason | Consequence amount and lineage | `money_eligibility` |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| Admission-sequenced run 1, no attestation, after late acceptance and settled tick | `INDETERMINATE`; `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`, `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` | New revision; proven Candidate spans `SUPPORTED`, material refused tail linked, `SERIES_LIMIT`; `OPEN_BOUND` only if independently true | Recorded 7.5625 revision and its exact old run/selection/Finding/consequence IDs retained; no current amount | `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` |
+| Same run 1, valid labelled simulated attestation recorded, before reassessment settles | Prior selection is stale; relevant S3/S4 work pending | Prior revision retained, new result pending | 7.5625 and all old IDs retained; no current amount | `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` |
+| Same run 1, reassessment settled under valid simulated attestation | Source-ordered full series; no generator-series refusal; late admission sequence is not inversion | New full-episode `SUPPORTED` revision with truthful non-series end reason | New exact-head 15.8125 revision with new run/selection/Finding/consequence IDs; old 7.5625 and IDs remain historical | `ELIGIBLE`, subject to exact-head `CURRENT PRICED` and source/currentness checks |
+| On-time-sequenced late upload with valid simulated attestation | Full ordered series, no generator-series refusal | Full-episode `SUPPORTED` revision, truthful end | Exact-head 15.8125 with all linked IDs | `ELIGIBLE`, subject to the same amount checks |
+| Simulated data without attestation; expired, conflicting, revoked, clock-step or genuine-overlap proof | Relevant typed series/overlap limit; no invented order | Material limit yields revised `SERIES_LIMIT`; pending work takes precedence | Preserve previous revisions/IDs; no current amount | `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` until settled, then `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` for material refusal |

+## Architect rulings
+
+Architect Q1-Q4 and the corrected overlap rule are accepted in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md` (including “Packet changes for the Planner” 1-3 and “Follow-up M1”) and decision `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. Those rulings govern implementation and proof above.
 
-1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+**Plan fix round 1:** Applied the Architect's placement/overlap and requeue rulings; fixed the attestation authority, append-only as-of path and eligibility precedence; added exact outcome and transition proof across S3, Finding and Financials; made the owner UI serving and browser evidence reachable; corrected citations, test targets, simulated wording and cross-midnight window scope, with expected model/matcher revision churn called out.
 
 **Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

 Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

+Architect Q1-Q4 and the corrected overlap rule are accepted in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md` (including “Packet changes for the Planner” 1-3 and “Follow-up M1”) and decision `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. Those rulings govern implementation and proof above.
 
-1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.

+## Architect-ruled contract — S3 cohort v4
 
-Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+The reviewed contract is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

+## Architect-ruled contract — S3 cohort v4
 
-Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+The reviewed contract is deterministic and fail closed:
 
-1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
-3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

 ## Phases, proof and review
 
+The same outcome table is the acceptance oracle for both phases. IDs below mean exact persisted IDs, never only a count or a currentness label; 7.5625 and 15.8125 are USD consequence amounts for the authored fixture.

+| Input / transition | S3 status and limits | Finding revision / end reason | Consequence amount and lineage | `money_eligibility` |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| Admission-sequenced run 1, no attestation, after late acceptance and settled tick | `INDETERMINATE`; `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`, `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` | New revision; proven Candidate spans `SUPPORTED`, material refused tail linked, `SERIES_LIMIT`; `OPEN_BOUND` only if independently true | Recorded 7.5625 revision and its exact old run/selection/Finding/consequence IDs retained; no current amount | `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` |
+| Same run 1, valid labelled simulated attestation recorded, before reassessment settles | Prior selection is stale; relevant S3/S4 work pending | Prior revision retained, new result pending | 7.5625 and all old IDs retained; no current amount | `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` |
+| Same run 1, reassessment settled under valid simulated attestation | Source-ordered full series; no generator-series refusal; late admission sequence is not inversion | New full-episode `SUPPORTED` revision with truthful non-series end reason | New exact-head 15.8125 revision with new run/selection/Finding/consequence IDs; old 7.5625 and IDs remain historical | `ELIGIBLE`, subject to exact-head `CURRENT PRICED` and source/currentness checks |
+| On-time-sequenced late upload with valid simulated attestation | Full ordered series, no generator-series refusal | Full-episode `SUPPORTED` revision, truthful end | Exact-head 15.8125 with all linked IDs | `ELIGIBLE`, subject to the same amount checks |
+| Simulated data without attestation; expired, conflicting, revoked, clock-step or genuine-overlap proof | Relevant typed series/overlap limit; no invented order | Material limit yields revised `SERIES_LIMIT`; pending work takes precedence | Preserve previous revisions/IDs; no current amount | `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` until settled, then `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` for material refusal |

-1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.

-1. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
-2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.

+2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) ± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. -1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
-2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
-3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
-4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
